Title: Three Letters concerning the Present State of Italy, written in the year 1687: I. relating to the affair of Molinos and the Quietists; II. relating to the Inquisition and the state of religion; III. relating to the policy and interests of some of the states of Italy; being a supplement to Dr. Burnet's Letters
Creator: Gilbert Burnet (1643-1715), per the host's catalogue record; the title page names no author
Publisher: No place or printer on the title page; 'Printed in the Year 1688'
Publication Date: 1688
Rights: Public Domain (published 1688, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item threelettersconc00burn (https://archive.org/details/threelettersconc00burn), the item's own threelettersconc00burn_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Boston Public Library; host metadata date: 1688. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 1a701d6c13bfaf4a3108936981b52ffe6d8a2a5f9a9bc55243425f023f41d065.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Boston Public Library scan; clean OCR after a few garbled lines on the flyleaf.
Content note: An English Protestant traveller's report from Italy in 1687; the first letter is an account of Molinos, the Quietists and the Inquisition's proceedings against them, as the writer gathered it in Italy that year.
